(optional)\"\n94\t              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n95\t              value={memo}\n96\t              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n97\t            />\n98\t\n99\t            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n100\t              <button\n101\t                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n102\t                disabled={isPending}\n103\t                onClick={handleApprove}\n104\t              >\n105\t                Approve\n106\t              </button>\n107\t              <button\n108\t                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n109\t                disabled={isPending}\n110\t                onClick={handlePay}\n111\t              >\n112\t                Send Payout\n113\t              </button>\n114\t            </div>\n115\t          </>\n116\t        )}\n117\t\n118\t        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n119\t        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n120\t          {(payouts ?? `${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync, isPending } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC.address,\n        abi: USDC.abi,\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [PAYOUTS.address, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6)],\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: PAYOUTS.address,\n        abi: PAYOUTS.abi,\n        functionName: \"pay\",\n        args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6), memo],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n              {\" paid \"}\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>\n              {\" \"}\n              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","numLines":138,"startLine":1,"totalLines":138}}}

await publicClient?.getBlock({ blockHash: log.blockHash }) : null,\n        transactionData:\n          Options?.transactionData && log.transactionHash\n            ? await publicClient?.getTransaction({ hash: log.transactionHash })\n            : null,\n        receiptData:\n          Options?.receiptData && log.transactionHash\n            ? await publicClient?.getTransactionReceipt({ hash: log.transactionHash })\n            : null,\n      };\n    }),\n  );\n\n  return finalEvents;\n};\n\n/**\n * @deprecated **Recommended only for local (hardhat/anvil) chains and development.**\n * It uses getLogs which can overload RPC endpoints (especially on L2s with short block times).\n * For production, use an indexer such as ponder.sh or similar to query contract events efficiently.\n *\n * Reads events from a deployed contract.\n * @param config - The config settings\n * @param config.contractName - deployed contract name\n * @param config.eventName - name of the event to listen for\n * @param config.fromBlock - optional block number to start reading events from (defaults to `deployedOnBlock` in deployedContracts.ts if set for contract, otherwise defaults to 0)\n * @param config.toBlock - optional block number to stop reading events at (if not provided, reads until current block)\n * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n * @param config.filters - filters to be applied to the event (parameterName: value)\n * @param config.blockData - if set to true it will return the block data for each event (default: false)\n * @param config.transactionData - if set to true it will return the transaction data for each event (default: false)\n * @param config.receiptData - if set to true it will return the receipt data for each event (default: false)\n * @param config.watch - if set to true, the events will be updated every pollingInterval milliseconds set at scaffoldConfig (default: false)\n * @param config.enabled - set this to false to disable the hook from running (default: true)\n * @param config.blocksBatchSize - optional batch size for fetching events. If specified, each batch will contain at most this many blocks (default: 500)\n */\nexport const useScaffoldEventHistory = <\n  TContractName extends ContractName,\n  TEventName extends ExtractAbiEventNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>>,\n  TBlockData extends boolean = false,\n=== index ===\nexport * from \"./useContractLogs\";\nexport * from \"./useCopyToClipboard\";\nexport * from {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YGDTMdUNZ2My5VNQNmJ1Cv","type":"tool_result","content":"packages/nextjs/components/Header.tsx:26:    label: 'Payouts',\npackages/nextjs/components/Header.tsx:27:    href: '/payouts'\n===Header===\n\"use client\";\n\nimport React, { useRef } from \"react\";\nimport Image from \"next/image\";\nimport Link from \"next/link\";\nimport { usePathname } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { hardhat } from \"viem/chains\";\nimport { Bars3Icon, BugAntIcon } from \"@heroicons/react/24/outline\";\nimport { FaucetButton, RainbowKitCustomConnectButton } from \"~~/components/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { useOutsideClick, useTargetNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\n\n\ntype HeaderMenuLink = {\n  label: string;\n  href: string;\n  icon?: React.ReactNode;\n};\n\nexport const menuLinks: HeaderMenuLink[] = [\n  {\n    label: 'Home',\n    href: '/'\n  },\n  {\n    label: 'Payouts',\n    href: '/payouts'\n  },\n  {\n    label: 'Debug Contracts',\n    href: '/debug',\n    icon: <BugAntIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n  }\n];\n\nexport const HeaderMenuLinks = () => {\n  const pathname = usePathname();\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      {menuLinks.map(({ label, href, icon }) => {\n        const isActive = pathname === href;\n        return (\n          <li key={href} className=\"h-full\">\n            <Link\n              href={href}\n              passHref\n              className={`${\n                isActive ? \"bg-base-300\" : \"\"\n              } hover:bg-base-300 focus:!bg-base-300 h-full px-4 text-sm gap-2 flex items-center whitespace-nowrap`}\n            >\n              {icon}\n              <span>{label}</span>\n            </Link>\n          </li>\n        );\n      })}\n    </>\n  );\n};","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"43665f07-0880-4aad-a0e4-f385f3252016","uuid":"14efaed5-cf07-434c-a5c8-9fdb62666245","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:29:42.522Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"packages/nextjs/components/Header.tsx:26:    label: 'Payouts',\npackages/nextjs/components/Header.tsx:27:    href: '/payouts'\n===Header===\n\"use client\";\n\nimport React, { useRef } from \"react\";\nimport Image from \"next/image\";\nimport Link from \"next/link\";\nimport { usePathname } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { hardhat } from \"viem/chains\";\nimport { Bars3Icon, BugAntIcon } from \"@heroicons/react/24/outline\";\nimport { FaucetButton, RainbowKitCustomConnectButton } from \"~~/components/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { useOutsideClick, useTargetNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\n\n\ntype HeaderMenuLink = {\n  label: string;\n  href: string;\n  icon?: React.ReactNode;\n};\n\nexport const menuLinks: HeaderMenuLink[] = [\n  {\n    label: 'Home',\n    href: '/'\n  },\n  {\n    label: 'Payouts',\n    href: '/payouts'\n  },\n  {\n    label: 'Debug Contracts',\n    href: '/debug',\n    icon: <BugAntIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n  }\n];\n\nexport const HeaderMenuLinks = () => {\n  const pathname = usePathname();\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      {menuLinks.map(({ label, href, icon }) => {\n        const isActive = pathname === href;\n        return (\n          <li key={href} className=\"h-full\">\n            <Link\n              href={href}\n              passHref\n              className={`${\n                isActive ? {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SeMbXiWMm9oionFK5NyN9n","type":"tool_result","content":"  TBlockData extends boolean = false,\n  TTransactionData extends boolean = false,\n  TReceiptData extends boolean = false,\n>({\n  contractName,\n  eventName,\n  fromBlock,\n  toBlock,\n  chainId,\n  filters,\n  blockData,\n  transactionData,\n  receiptData,\n  watch,\n  enabled = true,\n  blocksBatchSize = 500,\n}: UseScaffoldEventHistoryConfig<TContractName, TEventName, TBlockData, TTransactionData, TReceiptData>) => {\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n\n  // Runtime warning for non-local chains\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (selectedNetwork.id !== hardhat.id) {\n      console.log(\n        \"⚠️ useScaffoldEventHistory is not optimized for production use. It can overload RPC endpoints (especially on L2s)\",\n      );\n    }\n  }, [selectedNetwork.id]);\n\n  const publicClient = usePublicClient({\n    chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n  });\n  const [liveEvents, setLiveEvents] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [lastFetchedBlock, setLastFetchedBlock] = useState<bigint | null>(null);\n  const [isPollingActive, setIsPollingActive] = useState(false);\n\n  const { data: blockNumber } = useBlockNumber({ watch: watch, chainId: selectedNetwork.id });\n\n  const { data: deployedContractData } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n    contractName,\n    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n  });\n\n  const event =\n    deployedContractData &&\n    ((deployedContractData.abi as Abi).find(part => part.type === \"event\" && part.name === eventName) as AbiEvent);\n\n  const isContractAddressAndClientReady = Boolean(deployedContractData?.address) && Boolean(publicClient);\n\n  const fromBlockValue =\n    fromBlock !== undefined\n      ? fromBlock\n      : BigInt(\n          deployedContractData && \"deployedOnBlock\" in deployedContractData\n            ? deployedContractData.deployedOnBlock || 0\n            : 0,\n        );\n\n  const query = useInfiniteQuery({\n    queryKey: [\n      \"eventHistory\",\n      {\n        contractName,\n        address: deployedContractData?.address,\n        eventName,\n        fromBlock: fromBlockValue?.toString(),\n        toBlock: toBlock?.toString(),\n        chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n        filters: JSON.stringify(filters, replacer),\n        blocksBatchSize: blocksBatchSize.toString(),\n      },\n    ],\n    queryFn: async ({ pageParam }) => {\n      if (!isContractAddressAndClientReady) return undefined;\n\n      // Calculate the toBlock for this batch\n      let batchToBlock = toBlock;\n      const batchEndBlock = pageParam + BigInt(blocksBatchSize) - 1n;\n      const maxBlock = toBlock || (blockNumber ? BigInt(blockNumber) : undefined);\n      if (maxBlock) {\n        batchToBlock = batchEndBlock < maxBlock ? batchEndBlock : maxBlock;\n      }\n\n      const data = await getEvents(\n        {\n          address: deployedContractData?.address,\n          event,\n          fromBlock: pageParam,\n          toBlock: batchToBlock,\n          args: filters,\n        },\n        publicClient,\n        { blockData, transactionData, receiptData },\n      );\n\n      setLastFetchedBlock(batchToBlock || blockNumber || 0n);\n\n      return data;\n    },\n    enabled: enabled && isContractAddressAndClientReady && !isPollingActive, // Disable when polling starts\n    initialPageParam: fromBlockValue,\n    getNextPageParam: (lastPage, allPages, lastPageParam) => {\n      if (!blockNumber || fromBlockValue >= blockNumber) return undefined;\n\n      const nextBlock = lastPageParam + BigInt(blocksBatchSize);\n\n      // Don't go beyond the specified toBlock or current block\n      const maxBlock = toBlock && toBlock < blockNumber ? toBlock : blockNumber;\n\n      if (nextBlock > maxBlock) return undefined;\n\n      return nextBlock;\n    },\n    select: data => {\n      const events = data.pages.flat() as unknown as UseScaffoldEventHistoryData<\n        TContractName,\n        TEventName,\n        TBlockData,\n        TTransactionData,\n        TReceiptData\n      >;\n\n      return {\n        pages: events?.reverse(),\n        pageParams: data.pageParams,\n      };\n    },\n  });\n\n  // Check if we're caught up and should start polling\n  const shouldStartPolling = () => {\n    if (!watch || !blockNumber || isPollingActive) return false;\n\n    return !query.hasNextPage && query.status === \"success\";\n  };\n\n  // Poll for new events when watch mode is enabled\n  useQuery({\n    queryKey: [\"liveEvents\", contractName, eventName, blockNumber?.toString(), lastFetchedBlock?.toString()],\n    enabled: Boolean(\n      watch && enabled && isContractAddressAndClientReady && blockNumber && (shouldStartPolling() || isPollingActive),\n    ),\n    queryFn: async () => {\n      if (!isContractAddressAndClientReady || !blockNumber) return null;\n\n      if (!isPollingActive && shouldStartPolling()) {\n        setIsPollingActive(true);\n      }\n\n      const maxBlock = toBlock && toBlock < blockNumber ? toBlock : blockNumber;\n      const startBlock = lastFetchedBlock || maxBlock;\n\n      // Only fetch if there are new blocks to check\n      if (startBlock >= maxBlock) return null;\n\n      const newEvents = await getEvents(\n        {\n          address: deployedContractData?.address,\n          event,\n          fromBlock: startBlock + 1n,\n          toBlock: maxBlock,\n          args: filters,","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"43665f07-0880-4aad-a0e4-f385f3252016","uuid":"ed35c6c6-ee16-4e49-b9ff-dc8151eb583d","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:29:49.360Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"  TBlockData extends boolean = false,\n  TTransactionData extends boolean = false,\n  TReceiptData extends boolean = false,\n>({\n  contractName,\n  eventName,\n  fromBlock,\n  toBlock,\n  chainId,\n  filters,\n  blockData,\n  transactionData,\n  receiptData,\n  watch,\n  enabled = true,\n  blocksBatchSize = 500,\n}: UseScaffoldEventHistoryConfig<TContractName, TEventName, TBlockData, TTransactionData, TReceiptData>) => {\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n\n  // Runtime warning for non-local chains\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (selectedNetwork.id !== hardhat.id) {\n      console.log(\n        \"⚠️ useScaffoldEventHistory is not optimized for production use. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VbwTznhKdCaBHBcVvu52rY","type":"tool_result","content":"import { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { MutateOptions } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport { Abi, ExtractAbiFunctionNames } from \"abitype\";\nimport { Config, UseWriteContractParameters, useAccount, useConfig, useWriteContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { WriteContractErrorType, WriteContractReturnType } from \"wagmi/actions\";\nimport { WriteContractVariables } from \"wagmi/query\";\nimport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useTransactor } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { AllowedChainIds, notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\nimport {\n  ContractAbi,\n  ContractName,\n  ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n  ScaffoldWriteContractVariables,\n  UseScaffoldWriteConfig,\n  simulateContractWriteAndNotifyError,\n} from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n\ntype ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName extends ContractName> = Omit<\n  ReturnType<typeof useWriteContract>,\n  \"writeContract\" | \"writeContractAsync\"\n> & {\n  isMining: boolean;\n  writeContractAsync: <\n    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n  >(\n    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n    options?: ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n  ) => Promise<WriteContractReturnType | undefined>;\n  writeContract: <TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">>(\n    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n    options?: Omit<ScaffoldWriteContractOptions, \"onBlockConfirmation\" | \"blockConfirmations\">,\n  ) => void;\n};\n\nexport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  config: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>,\n): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName>;\n/**\n * @deprecated Use object parameter version instead: useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"YourContract\" })\n */\nexport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  contractName: TContractName,\n  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName>;\n\n/**\n * Wrapper around wagmi's useWriteContract hook which automatically loads (by name) the contract ABI and address from\n * the contracts present in deployedContracts.ts & externalContracts.ts corresponding to targetNetworks configured in scaffold.config.ts\n * @param contractName - name of the contract to be written to\n * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n * @param writeContractParams - wagmi's useWriteContract parameters\n */\nexport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  configOrName: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName> | TContractName,\n  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName> {\n  const finalConfig =\n    typeof configOrName === \"string\"\n      ? { contractName: configOrName, writeContractParams, chainId: undefined }\n      : (configOrName as UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>);\n  const { contractName, chainId, writeContractParams: finalWriteContractParams } = finalConfig;\n\n  const wagmiConfig = useConfig();\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (typeof configOrName === \"string\") {\n      console.warn(\n        \"Using `useScaffoldWriteContract` with a string parameter is deprecated. Please use the object parameter version instead.\",\n      );\n    }\n  }, [configOrName]);\n\n  const { chain: accountChain } = useAccount();\n  const writeTx = useTransactor();\n  const [isMining, setIsMining] = useState(false);\n\n  const wagmiContractWrite = useWriteContract(finalWriteContractParams);\n\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n\n  const { data: deployedContractData } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n    contractName,\n    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n  });\n\n  const sendContractWriteAsyncTx = async <\n    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n  >(\n    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdM86m8JrZpAa7cnzRrYM","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PJZ4MmhkwaUyWNSsPSKU6u","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T152853Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\nconst PAYOUTS_ADDRESS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts.address;\n\n// Parse a USDC amount string to base units, returning null when it isn't a valid number.\nconst parseAmount = (value: string) => {\n  if (!value) return null;\n  try {\n    return parseUnits(value, USDC_DECIMALS);\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n};\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveAsync, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n  });\n  const { writeContractAsync: payAsync, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS_ADDRESS],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const parsedAmount = parseAmount(amount);\n  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount !== null && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const needsApproval = parsedAmount !== null && (allowance === undefined || allowance < parsedAmount);\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!hasAmount || parsedAmount === null) return;\n    await approveAsync({\n      functionName: \"approve\",\n      args: [PAYOUTS_ADDRESS, parsedAmount],\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (!isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || parsedAmount === null) return;\n    await payAsync({\n      functionName: \"pay\",\n      args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo],\n    });\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setMemo(\"\");\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isApproving ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isApproving || !hasAmount || !needsApproval}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPaying ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPaying || !isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || needsApproval}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n\n            {recipient && !isValidRecipient && (\n              <p className=\"text-error text-sm mt-2\">Enter a valid recipient address.</p>\n            )}\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={`${payout.transactionHash}-${i}`} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2\">\n                <Address address={payout.args.from} />\n                <span className=\"opacity-70\">paid</span>\n                <Address address={payout.args.to} />\n                <span>{payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC</span>\n              </div>\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":223,"cache_read_input_tokens":49749,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":223},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"43665f07-0880-4aad-a0e4-f385f3252016","uuid":"d5a12ca3-1595-4233-915a-d6a5635ea804","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:32:19.341Z","request_id":"req_011CdM86jLA66ZZAYBb9zhWT"}

`${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync, isPending } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC.address,\n        abi: USDC.abi,\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [PAYOUTS.address, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6)],\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: PAYOUTS.address,\n        abi: PAYOUTS.abi,\n        functionName: \"pay\",\n        args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6), memo],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n              {\" paid \"}\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>\n              {\" \"}\n              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","userModified":false}}

= 6;\nconst PAYOUTS_ADDRESS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts.address;\n\n// Parse a USDC amount string to base units, returning null when it isn't a valid number.\nconst parseAmount = (value: string) => {\n  if (!value) return null;\n  try {\n    return parseUnits(value, USDC_DECIMALS);\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n};\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveAsync, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n  });\n  const { writeContractAsync: payAsync, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS_ADDRESS],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const parsedAmount = parseAmount(amount);\n  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount !== null && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const needsApproval = parsedAmount !== null && (allowance === undefined || allowance < parsedAmount);\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!hasAmount || parsedAmount === null) return;\n    await approveAsync({\n      functionName: \"approve\",\n      args: [PAYOUTS_ADDRESS, parsedAmount],\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (!isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || parsedAmount === null) return;\n    await payAsync({\n      functionName: \"pay\",\n      args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo],\n    });\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setMemo(\"\");\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isApproving ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isApproving || !hasAmount || !needsApproval}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPaying ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPaying || !isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || needsApproval}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n\n            {recipient && !isValidRecipient && (\n              <p className=\"text-error text-sm mt-2\">Enter a valid recipient address.</p>\n            )}\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={`${payout.transactionHash}-${i}`} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2\">\n                <Address address={payout.args.from} />\n                <span className=\"opacity-70\">paid</span>\n                <Address address={payout.args.to} />\n                <span>{payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC</span>\n              </div>\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":69,"oldLines":7,"newStart":69,"newLines":7,"lines":["     if (!isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || parsedAmount === null) return;","     await payAsync({","       functionName: \"pay\",","-      args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo],","+      args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parsedAmount, memo],","     });","     setAmount(\"\");","     setMemo(\"\");"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

});\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const parsedAmount = parseAmount(amount);\n  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount !== null && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const needsApproval = parsedAmount !== null && (allowance === undefined || allowance < parsedAmount);\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!hasAmount || parsedAmount === null) return;\n    await approveAsync({\n      functionName: \"approve\",\n      args: [PAYOUTS_ADDRESS, parsedAmount],\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (!isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || parsedAmount === null) return;\n    await payAsync({\n      functionName: \"pay\",\n      args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parsedAmount, memo],\n    });\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setMemo(\"\");\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isApproving ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isApproving || !hasAmount || !needsApproval}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPaying ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPaying || !isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || needsApproval}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n\n            {recipient && !isValidRecipient && (\n              <p className=\"text-error text-sm mt-2\">Enter a valid recipient address.</p>\n            )}\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={`${payout.transactionHash}-${i}`} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2\">\n                <Address address={payout.args.from} chain={targetNetwork} />\n                <span className=\"opacity-70\">paid</span>\n                <Address address={payout.args.to} chain={targetNetwork} />\n                <span>{payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC</span>\n              </div>\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","numLines":163,"startLine":1,"totalLines":163}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01J1zsLYREMXqwg6RvJiLnFJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\n2\timport { useInfiniteQuery, useQuery } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\n3\timport { Abi, AbiEvent, ExtractAbiEventNames } from \"abitype\";\n4\timport { BlockNumber, GetLogsParameters } from \"viem\";\n5\timport { hardhat } from \"viem/chains\";\n6\timport { Config, UsePublicClientReturnType, useBlockNumber, usePublicClient } from \"wagmi\";\n7\timport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n8\timport { useDeployedContractInfo } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n9\timport { AllowedChainIds } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n10\timport { replacer } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/common\";\n11\timport {\n12\t  ContractAbi,\n13\t  ContractName,\n14\t  UseScaffoldEventHistoryConfig,\n15\t  UseScaffoldEventHistoryData,\n16\t} from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n17\t\n18\tconst getEvents = async (\n19\t  getLogsParams: GetLogsParameters<AbiEvent | undefined, AbiEvent[] | undefined, boolean, BlockNumber, BlockNumber>,\n20\t  publicClient?: UsePublicClientReturnType<Config, number>,\n21\t  Options?: {\n22\t    blockData?: boolean;\n23\t    transactionData?: boolean;\n24\t    receiptData?: boolean;\n25\t  },\n26\t) => {\n27\t  const logs = await publicClient?.getLogs({\n28\t    address: getLogsParams.address,\n29\t    fromBlock: getLogsParams.fromBlock,\n30\t    toBlock: getLogsParams.toBlock,\n31\t    args: getLogsParams.args,\n32\t    event: getLogsParams.event,\n33\t  });\n34\t  if (!logs) return undefined;\n35\t\n36\t  const finalEvents = await Promise.all(\n37\t    logs.map(async log => {\n38\t      return {\n39\t        ...log,\n40\t        blockData:\n41\t          Options?.blockData && log.blockHash ? await publicClient?.getBlock({ blockHash: log.blockHash }) : null,\n42\t        transactionData:\n43\t          Options?.transactionData && log.transactionHash\n44\t            ? await publicClient?.getTransaction({ hash: log.transactionHash })\n45\t            : null,\n46\t        receiptData:\n47\t          Options?.receiptData && log.transactionHash\n48\t            ? await publicClient?.getTransactionReceipt({ hash: log.transactionHash })\n49\t            : null,\n50\t      };\n51\t    }),\n52\t  );\n53\t\n54\t  return finalEvents;\n55\t};\n56\t\n57\t/**\n58\t * @deprecated **Recommended only for local (hardhat/anvil) chains and development.**\n59\t * It uses getLogs which can overload RPC endpoints (especially on L2s with short block times).\n60\t * For production, use an indexer such as ponder.sh or similar to query contract events efficiently.\n61\t *\n62\t * Reads events from a deployed contract.\n63\t * @param config - The config settings\n64\t * @param config.contractName - deployed contract name\n65\t * @param config.eventName - name of the event to listen for\n66\t * @param config.fromBlock - optional block number to start reading events from (defaults to `deployedOnBlock` in deployedContracts.ts if set for contract, otherwise defaults to 0)\n67\t * @param config.toBlock - optional block number to stop reading events at (if not provided, reads until current block)\n68\t * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n69\t * @param config.filters - filters to be applied to the event (parameterName: value)\n70\t * @param config.blockData - if set to true it will return the block data for each event (default: false)\n71\t * @param config.transactionData - if set to true it will return the transaction data for each event (default: false)\n72\t * @param config.receiptData - if set to true it will return the receipt data for each event (default: false)\n73\t * @param config.watch - if set to true, the events will be updated every pollingInterval milliseconds set at scaffoldConfig (default: false)\n74\t * @param config.enabled - set this to false to disable the hook from running (default: true)\n75\t * @param config.blocksBatchSize - optional batch size for fetching events. If specified, each batch will contain at most this many blocks (default: 500)\n76\t */\n77\texport const useScaffoldEventHistory = <\n78\t  TContractName extends ContractName,\n79\t  TEventName extends ExtractAbiEventNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>>,\n80\t  TBlockData extends boolean = false,\n81\t  TTransactionData extends boolean = false,\n82\t  TReceiptData extends boolean = false,\n83\t>({\n84\t  contractName,\n85\t  eventName,\n86\t  fromBlock,\n87\t  toBlock,\n88\t  chainId,\n89\t  filters,\n90\t  blockData,\n91\t  transactionData,\n92\t  receiptData,\n93\t  watch,\n94\t  enabled = true,\n95\t  blocksBatchSize = 500,\n96\t}: UseScaffoldEventHistoryConfig<TContractName, TEventName, TBlockData, TTransactionData, TReceiptData>) => {\n97\t  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n98\t\n99\t  // Runtime warning for non-local chains\n100\t  useEffect(() => {\n101\t    if (selectedNetwork.id !== hardhat.id) {\n102\t      console.log(\n103\t        \"⚠️ useScaffoldEventHistory is not optimized for production use. It can overload RPC endpoints (especially on L2s)\",\n104\t      );\n105\t    }\n106\t  }, [selectedNetwork.id]);\n107\t\n108\t  const publicClient = usePublicClient({\n109\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n110\t  });\n111\t  const [liveEvents, setLiveEvents] = useState<any[]>([]);\n112\t  const [lastFetchedBlock, setLastFetchedBlock] = useState<bigint | null>(null);\n113\t  const [isPollingActive, setIsPollingActive] = useState(false);\n114\t\n115\t  const { data: blockNumber } = useBlockNumber({ watch: watch, chainId: selectedNetwork.id });\n116\t\n117\t  const { data: deployedContractData } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n118\t    contractName,\n119\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n120\t  });\n121\t\n122\t  const event =\n123\t    deployedContractData &&\n124\t    ((deployedContractData.abi as Abi).find(part => part.type === \"event\" && part.name === eventName) as AbiEvent);\n125\t\n126\t  const isContractAddressAndClientReady = Boolean(deployedContractData?.address) && Boolean(publicClient);\n127\t\n128\t  const fromBlockValue =\n129\t    fromBlock !== undefined\n130\t      ? fromBlock\n131\t      : BigInt(\n132\t          deployedContractData && \"deployedOnBlock\" in deployedContractData\n133\t            ? deployedContractData.deployedOnBlock || 0\n134\t            : 0,\n135\t        );\n136\t\n137\t  const query = useInfiniteQuery({\n138\t    queryKey: [\n139\t      \"eventHistory\",\n140\t      {\n141\t        contractName,\n142\t        address: deployedContractData?.address,\n143\t        eventName,\n144\t        fromBlock: fromBlockValue?.toString(),\n145\t        toBlock: toBlock?.toString(),\n146\t        chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n147\t        filters: JSON.stringify(filters, replacer),\n148\t        blocksBatchSize: blocksBatchSize.toString(),\n149\t      },\n150\t    ],\n151\t    queryFn: async ({ pageParam }) => {\n152\t      if (!isContractAddressAndClientReady) return undefined;\n153\t\n154\t      // Calculate the toBlock for this batch\n155\t      let batchToBlock = toBlock;\n156\t      const batchEndBlock = pageParam + BigInt(blocksBatchSize) - 1n;\n157\t      const maxBlock = toBlock || (blockNumber ? BigInt(blockNumber) : undefined);\n158\t      if (maxBlock) {\n159\t        batchToBlock = batchEndBlock < maxBlock ? batchEndBlock : maxBlock;\n160\t      }\n161\t\n162\t      const data = await getEvents(\n163\t        {\n164\t          address: deployedContractData?.address,\n165\t          event,\n166\t          fromBlock: pageParam,\n167\t          toBlock: batchToBlock,\n168\t          args: filters,\n169\t        },\n170\t        publicClient,\n171\t        { blockData, transactionData, receiptData },\n172\t      );\n173\t\n174\t      setLastFetchedBlock(batchToBlock || blockNumber || 0n);\n175\t\n176\t      return data;\n177\t    },\n178\t    enabled: enabled && isContractAddressAndClientReady && !isPollingActive, // Disable when polling starts\n179\t    initialPageParam: fromBlockValue,\n180\t    getNextPageParam: (lastPage, allPages, lastPageParam) => {\n181\t      if (!blockNumber || fromBlockValue >= blockNumber) return undefined;\n182\t\n183\t      const nextBlock = lastPageParam + BigInt(blocksBatchSize);\n184\t\n185\t      // Don't go beyond the specified toBlock or current block\n186\t      const maxBlock = toBlock && toBlock < blockNumber ? toBlock : blockNumber;\n187\t\n188\t      if (nextBlock > maxBlock) return undefined;\n189\t\n190\t      return nextBlock;\n191\t    },\n192\t    select: data => {\n193\t      const events = data.pages.flat() as unknown as UseScaffoldEventHistoryData<\n194\t        TContractName,\n195\t        TEventName,\n196\t        TBlockData,\n197\t        TTransactionData,\n198\t        TReceiptData\n199\t      >;\n200\t\n201\t      return {\n202\t        pages: events?.reverse(),\n203\t        pageParams: data.pageParams,\n204\t      };\n205\t    },\n206\t  });\n207\t\n208\t  // Check if we're caught up and should start polling\n209\t  const shouldStartPolling = () => {\n210\t    if (!watch || !blockNumber || isPollingActive) return false;\n211\t\n212\t    return !query.hasNextPage && query.status === \"success\";\n213\t  };\n214\t\n215\t  // Poll for new events when watch mode is enabled\n216\t  useQuery({\n217\t    queryKey: [\"liveEvents\", contractName, eventName, blockNumber?.toString(), lastFetchedBlock?.toString()],\n218\t    enabled: Boolean(\n219\t      watch && enabled && isContractAddressAndClientReady && blockNumber && (shouldStartPolling() || isPollingActive),\n220\t    ),\n221\t    queryFn: async () => {\n222\t      if (!isContractAddressAndClientReady || !blockNumber) return null;\n223\t\n224\t      if (!isPollingActive && shouldStartPolling()) {\n225\t        setIsPollingActive(true);\n226\t      }\n227\t\n228\t      const maxBlock = toBlock && toBlock < blockNumber ? toBlock : blockNumber;\n229\t      const startBlock = lastFetchedBlock || maxBlock;\n230\t\n231\t      // Only fetch if there are new blocks to check\n232\t      if (startBlock >= maxBlock) return null;\n233\t\n234\t      const newEvents = await getEvents(\n235\t        {\n236\t          address: deployedContractData?.address,\n237\t          event,\n238\t          fromBlock: startBlock + 1n,\n239\t          toBlock: maxBlock,\n240\t          args: filters,\n241\t        },\n242\t        publicClient,\n243\t        { blockData, transactionData, receiptData },\n244\t      );\n245\t\n246\t      if (newEvents && newEvents.length > 0) {\n247\t        setLiveEvents(prev => [...newEvents, ...prev]);\n248\t      }\n249\t\n250\t      setLastFetchedBlock(maxBlock);\n251\t      return newEvents;\n252\t    },\n253\t    refetchInterval: false,\n254\t  });\n255\t\n256\t  // Manual trigger to fetch next page when previous page completes (only when not polling)\n257\t  useEffect(() => {\n258\t    if (\n259\t      !isPollingActive &&\n260\t      query.status === \"success\" &&\n261\t      query.hasNextPage &&\n262\t      !query.isFetchingNextPage &&\n263\t      !query.error\n264\t    ) {\n265\t      query.fetchNextPage();\n266\t    }\n267\t  }, [query, isPollingActive]);\n268\t\n269\t  // Combine historical data from infinite query with live events from watch hook\n270\t  const historicalEvents = query.data?.pages || [];\n271\t  const allEvents = [...liveEvents, ...historicalEvents] as typeof historicalEvents;\n272\t\n273\t  // remove duplicates\n274\t  const seenEvents = new Set<string>();\n275\t  const combinedEvents = allEvents.filter(event => {\n276\t    const eventKey = `${event?.transactionHash}-${event?.logIndex}-${event?.blockHash}`;\n277\t    if (seenEvents.has(eventKey)) {\n278\t      return false;\n279\t    }\n280\t    seenEvents.add(eventKey);\n281\t    return true;\n282\t  }) as typeof historicalEvents;\n283\t\n284\t  return {\n285\t    data: combinedEvents,\n286\t    status: query.status,\n287\t    error: query.error,\n288\t    isLoading: query.isLoading,\n289\t    isFetchingNewEvent: query.isFetchingNextPage,\n290\t    refetch: query.refetch,\n291\t  };\n292\t};\n293\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01HevN6xKwhoU6BboBK6SRUR","session_id":"43665f07-0880-4aad-a0e4-f385f3252016","uuid":"c3cbaa60-02b1-4c1c-ae7f-0446fd4316d7","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:33:48.374Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts page changes"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015N2cSV21sddV6ykuxxGadK","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\n2\timport { MutateOptions } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\n3\timport { Abi, ExtractAbiFunctionNames } from \"abitype\";\n4\timport { Config, UseWriteContractParameters, useAccount, useConfig, useWriteContract } from \"wagmi\";\n5\timport { WriteContractErrorType, WriteContractReturnType } from \"wagmi/actions\";\n6\timport { WriteContractVariables } from \"wagmi/query\";\n7\timport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n8\timport { useDeployedContractInfo, useTransactor } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n9\timport { AllowedChainIds, notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n10\timport {\n11\t  ContractAbi,\n12\t  ContractName,\n13\t  ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n14\t  ScaffoldWriteContractVariables,\n15\t  UseScaffoldWriteConfig,\n16\t  simulateContractWriteAndNotifyError,\n17\t} from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n18\t\n19\ttype ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName extends ContractName> = Omit<\n20\t  ReturnType<typeof useWriteContract>,\n21\t  \"writeContract\" | \"writeContractAsync\"\n22\t> & {\n23\t  isMining: boolean;\n24\t  writeContractAsync: <\n25\t    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n26\t  >(\n27\t    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n28\t    options?: ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n29\t  ) => Promise<WriteContractReturnType | undefined>;\n30\t  writeContract: <TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">>(\n31\t    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n32\t    options?: Omit<ScaffoldWriteContractOptions, \"onBlockConfirmation\" | \"blockConfirmations\">,\n33\t  ) => void;\n34\t};\n35\t\n36\texport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n37\t  config: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>,\n38\t): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName>;\n39\t/**\n40\t * @deprecated Use object parameter version instead: useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"YourContract\" })\n41\t */\n42\texport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n43\t  contractName: TContractName,\n44\t  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n45\t): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName>;\n46\t\n47\t/**\n48\t * Wrapper around wagmi's useWriteContract hook which automatically loads (by name) the contract ABI and address from\n49\t * the contracts present in deployedContracts.ts & externalContracts.ts corresponding to targetNetworks configured in scaffold.config.ts\n50\t * @param contractName - name of the contract to be written to\n51\t * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n52\t * @param writeContractParams - wagmi's useWriteContract parameters\n53\t */\n54\texport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n55\t  configOrName: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName> | TContractName,\n56\t  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n57\t): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName> {\n58\t  const finalConfig =\n59\t    typeof configOrName === \"string\"\n60\t      ? { contractName: configOrName, writeContractParams, chainId: undefined }\n61\t      : (configOrName as UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>);\n62\t  const { contractName, chainId, writeContractParams: finalWriteContractParams } = finalConfig;\n63\t\n64\t  const wagmiConfig = useConfig();\n65\t\n66\t  useEffect(() => {\n67\t    if (typeof configOrName === \"string\") {\n68\t      console.warn(\n69\t        \"Using `useScaffoldWriteContract` with a string parameter is deprecated. Please use the object parameter version instead.\",\n70\t      );\n71\t    }\n72\t  }, [configOrName]);\n73\t\n74\t  const { chain: accountChain } = useAccount();\n75\t  const writeTx = useTransactor();\n76\t  const [isMining, setIsMining] = useState(false);\n77\t\n78\t  const wagmiContractWrite = useWriteContract(finalWriteContractParams);\n79\t\n80\t  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n81\t\n82\t  const { data: deployedContractData } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n83\t    contractName,\n84\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n85\t  });\n86\t\n87\t  const sendContractWriteAsyncTx = async <\n88\t    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n89\t  >(\n90\t    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n91\t    options?: ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n92\t  ) => {\n93\t    if (!deployedContractData) {\n94\t      notification.error(\"Target Contract is not deployed, did you forget to run `yarn deploy`?\");\n95\t      return;\n96\t    }\n97\t\n98\t    if (!accountChain?.id) {\n99\t      notification.error(\"Please connect your wallet\");\n100\t      return;\n101\t    }\n102\t\n103\t    if (accountChain?.id !== selectedNetwork.id) {\n104\t      notification.error(`Wallet is connected to the wrong network. Please switch to ${selectedNetwork.name}`);\n105\t      return;\n106\t    }\n107\t\n108\t    try {\n109\t      setIsMining(true);\n110\t      const { blockConfirmations, onBlockConfirmation, ...mutateOptions } = options || {};\n111\t\n112\t      const writeContractObject = {\n113\t        abi: deployedContractData.abi as Abi,\n114\t        address: deployedContractData.address,\n115\t        ...variables,\n116\t      } as WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>;\n117\t\n118\t      if (!finalConfig?.disableSimulate) {\n119\t        await simulateContractWriteAndNotifyError({\n120\t          wagmiConfig,\n121\t          writeContractParams: writeContractObject,\n122\t          chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n123\t        });\n124\t      }\n125\t\n126\t      const makeWriteWithParams = () =>\n127\t        wagmiContractWrite.writeContractAsync(\n128\t          writeContractObject,\n129\t          mutateOptions as\n130\t            | MutateOptions<\n131\t                WriteContractReturnType,\n132\t                WriteContractErrorType,\n133\t                WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>,\n134\t                unknown\n135\t              >\n136\t            | undefined,\n137\t        );\n138\t      const writeTxResult = await writeTx(makeWriteWithParams, { blockConfirmations, onBlockConfirmation });\n139\t\n140\t      return writeTxResult;\n141\t    } catch (e: any) {\n142\t      throw e;\n143\t    } finally {\n144\t      setIsMining(false);\n145\t    }\n146\t  };\n147\t\n148\t  const sendContractWriteTx = <\n149\t    TContractName extends ContractName,\n150\t    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n151\t  >(\n152\t    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n153\t    options?: Omit<ScaffoldWriteContractOptions, \"onBlockConfirmation\" | \"blockConfirmations\">,\n154\t  ) => {\n155\t    if (!deployedContractData) {\n156\t      notification.error(\"Target Contract is not deployed, did you forget to run `yarn deploy`?\");\n157\t      return;\n158\t    }\n159\t    if (!accountChain?.id) {\n160\t      notification.error(\"Please connect your wallet\");\n161\t      return;\n162\t    }\n163\t\n164\t    if (accountChain?.id !== selectedNetwork.id) {\n165\t      notification.error(`Wallet is connected to the wrong network. Please switch to ${selectedNetwork.name}`);\n166\t      return;\n167\t    }\n168\t\n169\t    wagmiContractWrite.writeContract(\n170\t      {\n171\t        abi: deployedContractData.abi as Abi,\n172\t        address: deployedContractData.address,\n173\t        ...variables,\n174\t      } as WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>,\n175\t      options as\n176\t        | MutateOptions<\n177\t            WriteContractReturnType,\n178\t            WriteContractErrorType,\n179\t            WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>,\n180\t            unknown\n181\t          >\n182\t        | undefined,\n183\t    );\n184\t  };\n185\t\n186\t  return {\n187\t    ...wagmiContractWrite,\n188\t    isMining,\n189\t    // Overwrite wagmi's writeContactAsync\n190\t    writeContractAsync: sendContractWriteAsyncTx,\n191\t    // Overwrite wagmi's writeContract\n192\t    writeContract: sendContractWriteTx,\n193\t  };\n194\t}\n195\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01HevN6xKwhoU6BboBK6SRUR","session_id":"43665f07-0880-4aad-a0e4-f385f3252016","uuid":"48519c41-11fe-4891-9718-357e484f751a","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:34:16.486Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts page changes"}

\"viem\";\n5\timport { useBlockNumber, useReadContract } from \"wagmi\";\n6\timport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n7\timport { useDeployedContractInfo } from address from\n18\t * the contracts present in deployedContracts.ts & externalContracts.ts corresponding to targetNetworks configured in scaffold.config.ts\n19\t * @param config - The config settings, including extra wagmi configuration\n20\t * @param config.contractName - deployed contract name\n21\t * @param config.functionName - name of the function to be called\n22\t * @param config.args - args to be passed to the function call\n23\t * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n24\t */\n25\texport const useScaffoldReadContract = <\n26\t  TContractName extends ContractName,\n27\t  TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"pure\" | \"view\">,\n28\t>({\n29\t  contractName,\n30\t  functionName,\n31\t  args,\n32\t  chainId,\n33\t  ...readConfig\n34\t}: UseScaffoldReadConfig<TContractName, TFunctionName>) => {\n35\t  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n36\t  const { data: deployedContract } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n37\t    contractName,\n38\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n39\t  });\n40\t\n41\t  const { query: queryOptions, watch, ...readContractConfig } = readConfig;\n42\t  // set watch to true by default\n43\t  const defaultWatch = watch ?? true;\n44\t\n45\t  const readContractHookRes = useReadContract({\n46\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n47\t    functionName,\n48\t    address: deployedContract?.address,\n49\t    abi: deployedContract?.abi,\n50\t    args,\n51\t    ...(readContractConfig as any),\n52\t    query: {\n53\t      enabled: !Array.isArray(args) || !args.some(arg => arg === undefined),\n54\t      ...queryOptions,\n55\t    },\n56\t  }) as Omit<ReturnType<typeof useReadContract>, \"data\" | \"refetch\"> & {\n57\t    data: AbiFunctionReturnType<ContractAbi, TFunctionName> | undefined;\n58\t    refetch: (\n59\t      options?: RefetchOptions | undefined,\n60\t    ) => Promise<QueryObserverResult<AbiFunctionReturnType<ContractAbi, TFunctionName>, ReadContractErrorType>>;\n61\t  };\n62\t\n63\t  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n64\t  const { data: blockNumber } = useBlockNumber({\n65\t    watch: defaultWatch,\n66\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n67\t    query: {\n68\t      enabled: defaultWatch,\n69\t    },\n70\t  });\n71\t\n72\t  useEffect(() => {\n73\t    if (defaultWatch) {\n74\t      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: readContractHookRes.queryKey });\n75\t    }\n76\t    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n77\t  }, [blockNumber]);\n78\t\n79\t  return {"type":"system","subtype":"task_notification","task_id":"aef1f3fa4cb6edece","tool_use_id":"toolu_01HevN6xKwhoU6BboBK6SRUR","status":"completed","output_file":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-liana-Documents-prog-buidl-guidl-ethskills-evals-artifacts-qa-goal-002-2026-07-24T152853Z-claude-no-skill-1-workspace/43665f07-0880-4aad-a0e4-f385f3252016/tasks/aef1f3fa4cb6edece.output","summary":"I have enough to give a precise review. Here are my findings.\n\n## Review: `packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx`\n\nOverall the rewrite is correct and idiomatic SE-2. Your three headline changes all check out:\n\n- Dropping `fromBlock` correctly falls through to `deployedOnBlock` (33712450) — confirmed in the hook at `useScaffoldEventHistory.ts:128-135`. Good call; `0n` on Base would page millions of 500-block batches.\n- Both writes go through `useScaffoldWriteContract` with correct `contractName`/`functionName`/`args` shapes. `approve(spender, amount)` and `pay(to, amount, memo)` arg tuples match the ABIs. `isMining` is a real field on the return type. No hook misuse.\n- `Address` `chain={targetNetwork}` prop matches usage everywhere else in the repo (e.g. `app/page.tsx:27`, `blockexplorer/...`). Fine.\n\nNo type errors found by inspection. The `args: [connectedAddress]` reads are safe: `useScaffoldReadContract.ts:53` disables the query when any arg is `undefined`, and the not-connected branch doesn't render anyway.\n\n### Concrete issues (all minor — nothing blocks shipping)\n\n1. **Unhandled promise rejections in `handleApprove`/`handlePay` (lines 66-82).** `writeContractAsync` rethrows on revert/user-rejection (see `useScaffoldWriteContract.ts:141-145`). You `await` without a `try/catch`, and `onClick={handleApprove}` discards the returned promise, so a rejected tx produces an uncaught-rejection in the console. Not user-facing — `useTransactor` still fires the error notification — but wrap the awaits in `try/catch` (or `.catch`) to keep it clean. Bonus: the current code only runs `setAmount(\"\")/setMemo(\"\")` on success, which is actually the right behavior, so keep that inside the try.\n\n2. **Invalid amount gives no feedback (lines 61-63, 136-138).** A non-numeric amount or one with >6 decimals makes `parseAmount` return `null` → `hasAmount` false → both buttons silently disabled. You show an inline error for a bad recipient but nothing for a bad amount. Consider a parallel `amount && !hasAmount` hint.\n\n3. **Balance display can't distinguish \"loading\" from \"zero\" (line 95).** `usdcBalance === undefined ? ... : \"0\"` renders `\"0\"` while the read is in flight. Cosmetic.\n\n4. **No balance-sufficiency gate.** Nothing stops `amount > balance`; you rely on the simulate/revert path to notify. Acceptable, but worth a conscious decision for real users.\n\n5. **`recipient` not cleared after a successful payout (lines 80-81).** You reset `amount` and `memo` but leave `recipient` populated. Minor inconsistency — intentional or not?\n\n6. **Hardcoded `deployedContracts[8453]` for `PAYOUTS_ADDRESS` (line 17)** while the hooks resolve address via `targetNetwork`. Consistent as long as this stays Base-only (Payouts only exists in 8453, so a wrong target network just yields a \"not deployed\" notification), but it's a latent footgun if the config ever changes. Fine for this week.\n\n### Non-issues I checked and cleared\n- `isAddress(recipient)` with viem 2.39.0: all-lowercase addresses still pass (the `toLowerCase() === address` short-circuit runs before the strict-checksum branch), so pasted lowercase recipients are not rejected. No bug.\n- Native USDC on Base has no approve-to-zero-first requirement, so the exact-amount `approve` is fine.\n- `needsApproval` uses `<` (line 64), so `allowance === amount` correctly permits paying without re-approving. Treating `allowance === undefined` (still loading) as \"needs approval\" is the safe default.\n- \"Latest payouts\" ordering is newest-first — the hook's `select` reverses pages (`useScaffoldEventHistory.ts:202`), matching your heading.\n\nRelevant files: `/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T152853Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx`, `/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T152853Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`","usage":{"total_tokens":32777,"tool_uses":12,"duration_ms":220422},"uuid":"dfe77751-b68b-439f-993d-af19efb1029b","session_id":"43665f07-0880-4aad-a0e4-f385f3252016"}

I just rewrote it.\n\nContext of my changes vs the original:\n- Original used `fromBlock: 0n` in useScaffoldEventHistory — on Base (block ~33.7M) that pages from 0 in 500-block batches, so I removed it to let the hook default to `deployedOnBlock` (33712450 in deployedContracts.ts).\n- Original used raw wagmi `useWriteContract` with explicit address/abi and swallowed errors to console. I switched both approve (USDC) and pay (Payouts) to `useScaffoldWriteContract` for network guard + notifications + confirmation wait.\n- Added input validation (isAddress, safe parseUnits), allowance-aware button gating, separate isMining states.\n\nFocus your review strictly on correctness and SE-2 conventions. Do NOT rewrite the file; report concrete issues only (bugs, type errors, hook misuse, wrong prop names, edge cases). Note: node_modules is not installed so I cannot run tsc/lint — check types by inspection. Both USDC and Payouts live in deployedContracts[8453]. Be concise.","agentId":"aef1f3fa4cb6edece","agentType":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I have enough to give a precise review. Here are my findings.\n\n## Review: `packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx`\n\nOverall the rewrite is correct and idiomatic SE-2. Your three headline changes all check out:\n\n- Dropping `fromBlock` correctly falls through to `deployedOnBlock` (33712450) — confirmed in the hook at `useScaffoldEventHistory.ts:128-135`. Good call; `0n` on Base would page millions of 500-block batches.\n- Both writes go through `useScaffoldWriteContract` with correct `contractName`/`functionName`/`args` shapes. `approve(spender, amount)` and `pay(to, amount, memo)` arg tuples match the ABIs. `isMining` is a real field on the return type. No hook misuse.\n- `Address` `chain={targetNetwork}` prop matches usage everywhere else in the repo (e.g. `app/page.tsx:27`, `blockexplorer/...`). Fine.\n\nNo type errors found by inspection. The `args: [connectedAddress]` reads are safe: `useScaffoldReadContract.ts:53` disables the query when any arg is `undefined`, and the not-connected branch doesn't render anyway.\n\n### Concrete issues (all minor — nothing blocks shipping)\n\n1. **Unhandled promise rejections in `handleApprove`/`handlePay` (lines 66-82).** `writeContractAsync` rethrows on revert/user-rejection (see `useScaffoldWriteContract.ts:141-145`). You `await` without a `try/catch`, and `onClick={handleApprove}` discards the returned promise, so a rejected tx produces an uncaught-rejection in the console. Not user-facing — `useTransactor` still fires the error notification — but wrap the awaits in `try/catch` (or `.catch`) to keep it clean. Bonus: the current code only runs `setAmount(\"\")/setMemo(\"\")` on success, which is actually the right behavior, so keep that inside the try.\n\n2. **Invalid amount gives no feedback (lines 61-63, 136-138).** A non-numeric amount or one with >6 decimals makes `parseAmount` return `null` → `hasAmount` false → both buttons silently disabled. You show an inline error for a bad recipient but nothing for a bad amount. Consider a parallel `amount && !hasAmount` hint.\n\n3. **Balance display can't distinguish \"loading\" from \"zero\" (line 95).** `usdcBalance === undefined ? ... : \"0\"` renders `\"0\"` while the read is in flight. Cosmetic.\n\n4. **No balance-sufficiency gate.** Nothing stops `amount > balance`; you rely on the simulate/revert path to notify. Acceptable, but worth a conscious decision for real users.\n\n5. **`recipient` not cleared after a successful payout (lines 80-81).** You reset `amount` and `memo` but leave `recipient` populated. Minor inconsistency — intentional or not?\n\n6. **Hardcoded `deployedContracts[8453]` for `PAYOUTS_ADDRESS` (line 17)** while the hooks resolve address via `targetNetwork`. Consistent as long as this stays Base-only (Payouts only exists in 8453, so a wrong target network just yields a \"not deployed\" notification), but it's a latent footgun if the config ever changes. Fine for this week.\n\n### Non-issues I checked and cleared\n- `isAddress(recipient)` with viem 2.39.0: all-lowercase addresses still pass (the `toLowerCase() === address` short-circuit runs before the strict-checksum branch), so pasted lowercase recipients are not rejected. No bug.\n- Native USDC on Base has no approve-to-zero-first requirement, so the exact-amount `approve` is fine.\n- `needsApproval` uses `<` (line 64), so `allowance === amount` correctly permits paying without re-approving. Treating `allowance === undefined` (still loading) as \"needs approval\" is the safe default.\n- \"Latest payouts\" ordering is newest-first — the hook's `select` reverses pages (`useScaffoldEventHistory.ts:202`), matching your heading.\n\nRelevant files: `/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T152853Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx`, `/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T152853Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`"}],"resolvedModel":"claude-opus-4-8","totalDurationMs":220423,"totalTokens":37223,"totalToolUseCount":12,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3053,"cache_read_input_tokens":29604,"output_tokens":4564,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3053},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":4564,"cache_read_input_tokens":29604,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3053,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3053,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"toolStats":{"readCount":5,"searchCount":7,"bashCount":0,"editFileCount":0,"linesAdded":0,"linesRemoved":0,"otherToolCount":0}}}
