setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch {}\n  };","originalFile":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport {\n  useScaffoldEventHistory,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n  useTargetNetwork,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\nconst PAYOUTS_ADDRESS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts.address;\n\n// Parse a USDC amount string to base units, returning null when it isn't a valid number.\nconst parseAmount = (value: string) => {\n  if (!value) return null;\n  try {\n    return parseUnits(value, USDC_DECIMALS);\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n};\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveAsync, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n  });\n  const { writeContractAsync: payAsync, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS_ADDRESS],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const parsedAmount = parseAmount(amount);\n  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount !== null && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const needsApproval = parsedAmount !== null && (allowance === undefined || allowance < parsedAmount);\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!hasAmount || parsedAmount === null) return;\n    await approveAsync({\n      functionName: \"approve\",\n      args: [PAYOUTS_ADDRESS, parsedAmount],\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (!isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || parsedAmount === null) return;\n    await payAsync({\n      functionName: \"pay\",\n      args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parsedAmount, memo],\n    });\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setMemo(\"\");\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isApproving ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isApproving || !hasAmount || !needsApproval}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPaying ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPaying || !isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || needsApproval}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n\n            {recipient && !isValidRecipient && (\n              <p className=\"text-error text-sm mt-2\">Enter a valid recipient address.</p>\n            )}\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport {\n  useScaffoldEventHistory,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n  useTargetNetwork,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\nconst PAYOUTS_ADDRESS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts.address;\n\n// Parse a USDC amount string to base units, returning null when it isn't a valid number.\nconst parseAmount = (value: string) => {\n  if (!value) return null;\n  try {\n    return parseUnits(value, USDC_DECIMALS);\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n};\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveAsync, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n  });\n  const { writeContractAsync: payAsync, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS_ADDRESS],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const parsedAmount = parseAmount(amount);\n  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount !== null && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const needsApproval = parsedAmount !== null && (allowance === undefined || allowance < parsedAmount);\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!hasAmount || parsedAmount === null) return;\n    // useScaffoldWriteContract already surfaces errors via notifications; catch to avoid an unhandled rejection.\n    try {\n      await approveAsync({\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [PAYOUTS_ADDRESS, parsedAmount],\n      });\n    } catch {}\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (!isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || parsedAmount === null) return;\n    try {\n      await payAsync({\n        functionName: \"pay\",\n        args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parsedAmount, memo],\n      });\n      setRecipient(\"\");\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch {}\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isApproving ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isApproving || !hasAmount || !needsApproval}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPaying ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPaying || !isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || needsApproval}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n\n            {recipient && !isValidRecipient && (\n              <p className=\"text-error text-sm mt-2\">Enter a valid recipient address.</p>\n            )}\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={`${payout.transactionHash}-${i}`} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2\">\n                <Address address={payout.args.from} chain={targetNetwork} />\n                <span className=\"opacity-70\">paid</span>\n                <Address address={payout.args.to} chain={targetNetwork} />\n                <span>{payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC</span>\n              </div>\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdM8h8DGdzRDKcjz7hhdz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Committed. Here's the final-pass summary.\n\n## What was broken → fixed\n\n**Critical — the feed never loaded**\n`useScaffoldEventHistory` was called with `fromBlock: 0n`. On Base (current block ~33.7M) the hook pages in 500-block batches, so that forced ~67,000 sequential RPC calls before the feed could render — it would hang and rate-limit the RPC. The contract already records `deployedOnBlock: 33712450`; removing the override lets the hook default to it.\n\n**Important — real-money write path had no guardrails**\nThe page used raw wagmi `useWriteContract` with hand-passed `address`/`abi`, and swallowed every error to `console.error`. Users got no target-network check, no transaction notifications, no confirmation wait, and silent failures. Switched both `approve` (USDC) and `pay` (Payouts) to `useScaffoldWriteContract` — the repo convention — which adds all of that. Split `isMining` per action so the two buttons no longer spin/disable together.\n\n**Correctness/UX**\n- Validate recipient (`isAddress`) and amount (safe `parseUnits`) before sending, with inline error hints — avoids gas wasted on reverting txs.\n- Allowance-aware gating: Approve enables only when approval is actually needed; Send enables only once allowance covers the amount.\n- Feed addresses now render via `@scaffold-ui` `Address` (ENS, avatar, explorer link) instead of a manual string slice; form clears on success.\n\n**Branding**\nTab/title metadata still said \"Scaffold-ETH 2 App\" — set to \"USDC Payouts\" for launch.\n\n## Left intentionally unchanged\n- **`Payouts.sol` / deploy script** — already deployed; `deployedContracts.ts` mirrors the live address, and USDC target (`0x8335...2913`) is consistent. Editing would desync from the deployed bytecode.\n- **`scaffold.config.ts` defaults** — shared Alchemy key / WalletConnect ID are read from env first (`NEXT_PUBLIC_*`); the deployer sets real values in Vercel. `pollingInterval: 30000` is the RPC-safe choice for an L2 event feed.\n\n## Note\n`node_modules` isn't installed in this workspace, so I couldn't run `yarn lint`/`tsc`/`build` — verified types and hook/prop shapes by inspection against the actual hook sources and existing `@scaffold-ui` usage, and confirmed with a code-review pass. Worth a `yarn install && yarn next:build` before the ship if you want a green build locally.\n\nOne open question: the home page (`app/page.tsx`) still shows default SE-2 landing content. I left it since the task scoped the deliverable to `/payouts` — want me to point the landing page at Payouts too?"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":397,"cache_read_input_tokens":74151,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":397},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"43665f07-0880-4aad-a0e4-f385f3252016","uuid":"c6ed28ee-9ece-46d1-aefd-138cf1e05287","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:39:30.146Z","request_id":"req_011CdM8h75JpSp3xfbNihYcr"}
